# [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/)통신 / 네트워크 키워드 목록 (1,200+ 심화 확장판)

정보통신기술사·컴퓨터응용시스템기술사 및 전문 엔지니어를 위한 [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/)통신/네트워크 전 영역 핵심 및 심화 키워드 1,200선입니다.

---

## 1. [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/)통신 기초 및 [신호](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/130_signal/)처리 (70개)

2. [정보처리장치](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/002_정보처리장치/) (Host Computer, Front-End Processor FEP)
3. [아날로그 신호](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/003_아날로그_신호_vs_디지털_신호/) (연속적, 증폭기) vs 디지털 [신호](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/130_signal/) (이산적, 리피터)
4. [배드보](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/004_배드보_비트레이트/) (Baud Rate, 변조 속도) vs [비트](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/02_data_representation_arithmetic/073_bit/)레이트 ([Bit](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/04_datastructure/086_fenwick_tree/) Rate, 전송 속도)
5. [비트 시간](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/005_비트_시간_심볼_시간/) ([Bit Duration](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/005_비트_시간_심볼_시간/)) / 심볼 시간 (Symbol Duration)
6. 아날로그 통신 vs 디지털 통신
7. [기저대역 전송](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/007_기저대역_대역통과_전송/) ([Baseband Transmission](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/007_기저대역_대역통과_전송/)) / 대역통과 전송 (Broadband Transmission)
8. [단방향](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/008_단방향_반이중_전이중/) ([Simplex](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/05_data_science/406_linear_programming_simplex/)) / 반이중 (Half-Duplex) / 전이중 (Full-Duplex)
9. [직렬 전송](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/009_직렬_전송_vs_병렬_전송/) ([Serial](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/009_직렬_전송_vs_병렬_전송/)) vs [병렬](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/07_exam_summary/430_index_fast_full_scan/) 전송 (Parallel)
[10](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/08_storage_and_io_systems/489_raid_10_hybrid/). [동기식 전송](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/010_동기식_비동기식_전송/) ([Synchronous](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/010_동기식_비동기식_전송/)) vs 비동기식 전송 (Asynchronous)
[11](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/308_static_dynamic_nat_pat_port_address_translation/). 비동기식 전송 - 시작/정지 [비트](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/02_data_representation_arithmetic/073_bit/) (Start/Stop [Bit](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/04_datastructure/086_fenwick_tree/)), [프레이밍](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/04_data_link_layer_error/184_framing_mechanism/) 에러
12. [동기식 전송](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/010_동기식_비동기식_전송/) - 문자 동기방식 (SYN, [BSC](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/01_governance_strategy/019_bsc/)), [비트](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/02_data_representation_arithmetic/073_bit/) 동기방식 ([SDLC](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/04_sdlc_testing/131_sdlc_system_development_life_cycle_waterfall_agile/), [HDLC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/04_data_link_layer_error/216_hdlc_high_level_data_link_control/))
13. [대역폭](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/140_bandwidth/) ([Bandwidth](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/140_bandwidth/)), [대역폭](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/140_bandwidth/)-효율성 [관계](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/02_modeling_normalization/083_relationship_in_er_model/)
14. [처리량](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/139_throughput/) ([Throughput](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/139_throughput/)) / 굿풋 (Goodput)
15. [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/) ([Latency](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/141_latency/)/Delay) - [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/) 관점
16. [전파 지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/016_전파_지연/) ([Propagation Delay](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/016_전파_지연/)) - 거리/속도
17. [전송 지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/017_전송_지연/) ([Transmission Delay](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/017_전송_지연/)) - 패킷길이/[대역폭](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/140_bandwidth/)
18. [큐잉 지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/018_큐잉_지연/) ([Queueing Delay](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/018_큐잉_지연/)) - 라우터 버퍼
19. [처리 지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/019_처리_지연/) ([Processing Delay](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/019_처리_지연/)) - 헤더 검사, [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/)
20. [나이퀴스트 채널 용량](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/020_나이퀴스트_채널_용량/) ([Nyquist Capacity](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/020_나이퀴스트_채널_용량/)) - 무잡음 채널, C = 2B log2(M)
21. [샤논의 채널 용량](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/021_샤논의_채널_용량/) ([Shannon Capacity](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/021_샤논의_채널_용량/)) - 잡음 채널, C = B log2(1 + S/N)
22. [심볼 상호 간섭](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/022_심볼_상호_간섭_ISI/) (ISI: Inter-Symbol Interference)
23. 나이퀴스트 펄스 포맷 / 아이패턴 (Eye Pattern)
24. [신호 대 잡음비](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/024_신호_대_잡음비/) ([SNR](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/024_신호_대_잡음비/), [Signal-to-Noise Ratio](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/024_신호_대_잡음비/))
25. 감쇠 (Attenuation), 데시벨(dB) 측정
26. [지연 왜곡](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/026_지연_왜곡/) ([Delay Distortion](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/026_지연_왜곡/))
27. 백색 잡음 (White Noise) / 가우스 잡음
28. 충격 잡음 (Impulse Noise) / 열 잡음 (Thermal Noise)
29. [상호변조 잡음](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/029_상호변조_잡음/) ([Intermodulation Noise](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/029_상호변조_잡음/))
30. [누화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/030_누화_크로스토크/) ([Crosstalk](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/030_누화_크로스토크/), 혼선)
31. [에코](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/031_에코_반향/) (Echo, [반향](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/031_에코_반향/))
32. [회선 제어 규약](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/032_회선_제어_규약/) (Line Discipline)
33. 엔트 (ENQ) / 애크 (ACK) / 나크 ([NAK](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/04_data_link_layer_error/211_nak_negative_acknowledgement/)) / EOT
34. [에러 검출율](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/034_에러_검출율/) ([Error Detection](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/01_overview_architecture/040_error_detection/) Rate)
35. 부호화 (Encoding) - Line Coding, [Block Coding](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/042_4B5B_8B10B_블록_코딩/)
36. Line Coding - 단극성 (Unipolar), 극성 (Polar), 양극성 (Bipolar)
37. NRZ (Non-[Return to Zero](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/038_RZ_Return_to_Zero/)) - NRZ-L, NRZ-I
38. RZ ([Return to Zero](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/038_RZ_Return_to_Zero/)) - [동기화](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/03_cpu_scheduling/212_synchronization_mechanisms/) 장점, [대역폭](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/140_bandwidth/) 증가
39. 맨체스터 (Manchester) 부호화 / 차분 맨체스터 (Differential Manchester)
40. [AMI](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/162_ami_advanced_metering_infrastructure/) (Alternate Mark Inversion) / Pseudoternary
41. [차분 부호화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/041_차분_부호화/) ([Differential Encoding](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/041_차분_부호화/))
42. 4B/5B, 8B/10B 부호화 ([Block Coding](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/042_4B5B_8B10B_블록_코딩/))
43. B8ZS (Bipolar with 8-[Zero](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/15_advanced_topics/585_zero_skipping/) Substitution) / HDB3 (High-Density Bipolar 3 zeros)
44. 변조 (Modulation) 필요성 - [안테나](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/03_physical_layer_media/171_antenna_basic_dipole_resonance/) 크기, 주파수 [다중화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/02_multiplexing_multiple_access/071_다중화_Multiplexing/)
45. 진폭 편이 변조 (ASK, [Amplitude Shift Keying](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/045_진폭_편이_변조_ASK/))
46. 주파수 편이 변조 (FSK, [Frequency Shift Keying](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/046_주파수_편이_변조_FSK/))
47. 위상 편이 변조 ([PSK](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/142_psk_pre_shared_key/), Phase Shift Keying)
48. BPSK (Binary [PSK](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/142_psk_pre_shared_key/), 1bit/symbol) / QPSK (Quadrature [PSK](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/142_psk_pre_shared_key/), 2bit/symbol)
49. OQPSK (Offset QPSK) / [Pi](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/01_governance_strategy/009_process_innovation/)/4 QPSK
50. M진 [PSK](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/142_psk_pre_shared_key/) (8PSK, 16PSK)
51. [직교 진폭 변조](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/051_직교_진폭_변조_QAM/) (QAM, Quadrature Amplitude Modulation) - ASK+[PSK](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/142_psk_pre_shared_key/) 혼합
52. 16-QAM, 64-QAM, 256-QAM, 1024-QAM
53. [성상도](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/053_성상도_Constellation_Diagram/) ([Constellation Diagram](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/053_성상도_Constellation_Diagram/)) - 진폭과 위상 표시

[RARP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/314_rarp_reverse_arp_mac_to_ip/) ([Reverse ARP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/314_rarp_reverse_arp_mac_to_ip/)) - MAC으로 IP 얻기 ([DHCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/10_application_layer_dns_mgmt/522_dhcp_dynamic_host_configuration_protocol/) 이전)
315. [Proxy ARP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/315_proxy_arp_subnet_proxy_response/) ([프록시](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/04_testing_quality/264_proxy_pattern_surrogate_access_control/) [ARP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/312_arp_address_resolution_protocol_ip_to_mac/)) - 다른 서브넷의 IP에 응답 대행
316. [Gratuitous ARP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/316_gratuitous_arp_g_arp_ip_conflict_cache_update/) ([G-ARP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/316_gratuitous_arp_g_arp_ip_conflict_cache_update/)) - 자신의 IP 충돌 감지 및 캐시 갱신 목적
317. [ARP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/312_arp_address_resolution_protocol_ip_to_mac/) 캐시 오염 ([ARP Cache Poisoning](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/318_arp_cache_poisoning/), [스푸핑](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/598_spoofing/) 공격)
318. [ICMP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/318_icmp_internet_control_message_protocol_diagnostics/) (Internet Control Message [Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/)) 진단/오류 알림
319. [ICMP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/318_icmp_internet_control_message_protocol_diagnostics/) [메시](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/10_parallel_processing_architecture/389_mesh_topology/)지 종류 - Query, Error Reporting
320. [Time Exceeded](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/320_icmp_time_exceeded_ttl_expiration_traceroute/) ([TTL](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/294_ttl_time_to_live_looping_prevention/) 만료, Traceroute 원리)
321. Destination Unreachable (목적지 도달 불가 - [포트](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/08_storage_and_io_systems/446_port_and_bus/), 호스트 차단)
322. Echo Request/Reply (Ping 원리) / Source Quench (혼잡 제어, 구형)
323. Redirect [메시](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/10_parallel_processing_architecture/389_mesh_topology/)지 - 더 나은 라우터 경로 통보
324. [IPv6](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/324_ipv6_128bit_next_generation_address/) (Internet [Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) Version 6) - 128비트 차세대 주소
325. [IPv6](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/324_ipv6_128bit_next_generation_address/) 단순화된 헤더 - 40바이트 고정 ([헤더 체크섬](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/296_header_checksum_ipv4_integrity/) 삭제, [단편화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/291_fragmentation_and_reassembly_process/) 삭제)
326. [트래픽 클래스](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/326_traffic_class_flow_label_ipv6_qos/) ([Traffic Class](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/326_traffic_class_flow_label_ipv6_qos/)) / 플로우 레이블 (Flow Label)
327. Next Header, 홉 제한 (Hop Limit, [TTL](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/294_ttl_time_to_live_looping_prevention/) 대응)
328. 유니캐스트, [멀티캐스트](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/298_ip_classes_a_b_c_d_multicast_e_experimental/), 애니캐스트(Anycast, 가장 가까운 노드 응답) - 브로드캐스트 없음
329. [링크 로컬 주소](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/329_ipv6_link_local_fe80_site_local/) ([IPv6](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/324_ipv6_128bit_next_generation_address/) Link Local, FE80::) / 사이트 로컬 주소
330. [EUI-64](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/330_eui_64_mac_to_ipv6_interface_id/) ([MAC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/673_mac_message_authentication_code/) 기반 [IPv6](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/324_ipv6_128bit_next_generation_address/) 호스트 주소 자동생성)
331. [SLAAC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/331_slaac_stateless_address_autoconfiguration_ndp/) ([Stateless Address Autoconfiguration](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/331_slaac_stateless_address_autoconfiguration_ndp/)) 무상태 주소 자동 [설정](/knowledge-base/studynote/15_devops_sre/01_culture_methodology/009_config/) - [NDP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/336_ndp_neighbor_discovery_protocol_ipv6/) 활용
332. [IPv4](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/286_ipv4_internet_protocol_version_4_rfc_791/)-[IPv6](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/324_ipv6_128bit_next_generation_address/) 전환 기술: 듀얼 [스택](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/04_datastructure/057_stack/) ([Dual Stack](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/332_ipv4_ipv6_transition_dual_stack_tunneling_nat64/)), [터널링](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/377_tunneling_mechanism_overview/) (ISATAP, 6to4), 주소 변환 (NAT64/DNS64)
333. [IGMP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/333_igmp_internet_group_management_protocol_multicast/) (Internet Group [Management](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/05_security_compliance/372_management/) [Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/)) - [IPv4](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/286_ipv4_internet_protocol_version_4_rfc_791/) [멀티캐스트](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/298_ip_classes_a_b_c_d_multicast_e_experimental/) 그룹 가입/탈퇴
334. [IGMP Snooping](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/334_igmp_snooping_multicast_traffic_control/) ([스위치](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/05_lan_wan_l2_devices/238_switch_operation_principles/)가 [멀티캐스트](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/298_ip_classes_a_b_c_d_multicast_e_experimental/) 트래픽 불필요한 [포트](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/08_storage_and_io_systems/446_port_and_bus/)에 차단)
335. [MLD](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/335_mld_multicast_listener_discovery_ipv6/) ([Multicast Listener Discovery](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/335_mld_multicast_listener_discovery_ipv6/)) - IGMP의 [IPv6](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/324_ipv6_128bit_next_generation_address/) [버전](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/288_version_ihl_tos_total_length/)
336. [NDP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/336_ndp_neighbor_discovery_protocol_ipv6/) ([Neighbor Discovery Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/336_ndp_neighbor_discovery_protocol_ipv6/)) - IPv6의 [ARP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/312_arp_address_resolution_protocol_ip_to_mac/)/[ICMP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/318_icmp_internet_control_message_protocol_diagnostics/) 대체, RS/[RA](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/161_ra_registration_authority/)/NS/NA 교환

## 7. 네트워크 계층 - [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/), [터널링](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/377_tunneling_mechanism_overview/), [QoS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/388_qos_quality_of_service_best_effort_intserv_diffserv/) (80개)

[라우터 구조 판단](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/337_router_architecture_rib_fib_control_data_plane/) - [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) 테이블(RIB), 포워딩 테이블([FIB](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/15_advanced_topics/781_fib_circuit_edit/)), 제어/[데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/) 평면

[CEF](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/338_cef_cisco_express_forwarding_hardware_switching/) ([Cisco Express Forwarding](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/338_cef_cisco_express_forwarding_hardware_switching/)) 물리적 포워딩 / 하드웨어 스위칭 ([ASIC](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/01_basic_electronics_logic/070_asic/))

[라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) ([Routing](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/)) 개요 - 최적 경로(Best Path) [설정](/knowledge-base/studynote/15_devops_sre/01_culture_methodology/009_config/)

[정적 라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/340_static_routing_default_route_0_0_0_0/) ([Static Routing](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/340_static_routing_default_route_0_0_0_0/)) - 관리자 수동 [설정](/knowledge-base/studynote/15_devops_sre/01_culture_methodology/009_config/) (지속성, [보안성](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/283_security_tactics/) 높음) / 디폴트 라우터 (0.0.0.0/0)

[동적 라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/341_dynamic_routing_protocol_operation/) ([Dynamic Routing](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/341_dynamic_routing_protocol_operation/)) - [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) 운영

[메트릭](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/342_routing_metric_hop_bandwidth_delay/) ([Metric](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/342_routing_metric_hop_bandwidth_delay/)) - 최적 경로 산출 기준 (홉 카운트, [대역폭](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/140_bandwidth/), [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/), 부하 등)

344. [AS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/344_as_autonomous_system_asn/) (Autonomous System, 자율 시스템) / ASN 분배
345. [IGP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/345_igp_interior_gateway_protocol_rip_ospf/) ([Interior Gateway Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/345_igp_interior_gateway_protocol_rip_ospf/)) - [AS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/344_as_autonomous_system_asn/) 내부 [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) ([RIP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/351_rip_routing_information_protocol_distance_vector_hop/), [OSPF](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/357_ospf_open_shortest_path_first_overview/), [EIGRP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/355_eigrp_enhanced_igrp_dual_algorithm/) 등)
346. [EGP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/346_egp_exterior_gateway_protocol_bgp/) ([Exterior Gateway Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/346_egp_exterior_gateway_protocol_bgp/)) - [AS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/344_as_autonomous_system_asn/) 외부간 [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) ([BGP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/365_bgp_border_gateway_protocol_path_vector/))

[거리 벡터](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/347_distance_vector_routing_bellman_ford/) ([Distance Vector](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/347_distance_vector_routing_bellman_ford/)) [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) [알고리즘](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/) - [벨만-포드](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/11_graph_algorithms/170_bellman_ford/)([Bellman-Ford](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/11_graph_algorithms/170_bellman_ford/)) 기반

[링크 상태](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/348_link_state_routing_dijkstra_spf/) ([Link State](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/348_link_state_routing_dijkstra_spf/)) [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) [알고리즘](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/) - [다익스트라](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/03_graph_search/036_dijkstra/)([Dijkstra](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/03_graph_search/036_dijkstra/)) 기반 (최단경로/[SPF](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/495_spf_sender_policy_framework/))

[RIP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/351_rip_routing_information_protocol_distance_vector_hop/) ([Routing Information Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/351_rip_routing_information_protocol_distance_vector_hop/)) - 거리벡터, 홉 카운트 [메트릭](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/342_routing_metric_hop_bandwidth_delay/)(최대 15), 30초 업데이트

[RIPv1](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/352_ripv1_classful_vs_ripv2_classless_vlsm/) (클래스풀, 브로드캐스트) vs RIPv2 ([클래스리스](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/303_cidr_classless_inter_domain_routing/)/[VLSM](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/306_vlsm_variable_length_subnet_mask/), [멀티캐스트](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/298_ip_classes_a_b_c_d_multicast_e_experimental/): 224.0.0.9)

[IGRP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/354_igrp_cisco_legacy_composite_metric/) - [Cisco](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/10_application_layer_dns_mgmt/539_netflow_sflow_traffic_monitoring/) 구형, 복합 [메트릭](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/342_routing_metric_hop_bandwidth_delay/) ([대역폭](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/140_bandwidth/)+[지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/) 등)

[OSPF](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/357_ospf_open_shortest_path_first_overview/) (Open [Shortest Path](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/07_exam_summary/547_graph_shortest_path_db_mapping/) First) - 대표적 [링크 상태](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/348_link_state_routing_dijkstra_spf/) [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/)

[OSPF](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/357_ospf_open_shortest_path_first_overview/) 인접성([Adjacency](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/358_ospf_adjacency_hello_lsa_lsdb/)), Hello 패킷, LSA ([Link State](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/348_link_state_routing_dijkstra_spf/) Advertisement), [LSDB](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/19_frequent_topics_terms/961_ospf_link_state_database_dijkstra_spf_routing/) 교환

420. CLOSE_WAIT / LAST_ACK 상태

[TCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/405_tcp_transmission_control_protocol_connection_oriented/) [흐름 제어](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/04_data_link_layer_error/213_flow_control_buffer_overflow/) ([Flow Control](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/421_tcp_flow_control_sliding_window_algorithm/)) - 수신자 관점, 슬라이딩 윈도우 [알고리즘](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/)
422. [윈도우 스케일옵션](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/422_tcp_window_scale_option/) ([Window Scale Option](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/422_tcp_window_scale_option/)) - 최대 1GB까지 윈도우 확장
423. [송신 버퍼](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/423_send_buffer_receive_buffer/) ([Send Buffer](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/423_send_buffer_receive_buffer/)) / 수신 버퍼 (Receive Buffer)
424. [어리석은 윈도우 증후군](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/424_silly_window_syndrome_problem/) ([Silly Window Syndrome](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/424_silly_window_syndrome_problem/)) 문제 
425. 네이글 [알고리즘](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/) (Nagle's [Algorithm](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/)) - 작은 패킷 [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/) 모음 (송신측 해결)
426. 클라크 해결책 (Clark's Solution) - 수신측 여유 전까지 [윈도우 크기](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/413_tcp_window_size_flow_control_16bit/) 0 유지 (수신측 해결)
427. [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/)된 ACK ([Delayed ACK](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/427_delayed_ack_tcp_optimization/)) - 응답 패킷 모아서 전송
428. [TCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/405_tcp_transmission_control_protocol_connection_oriented/) 혼잡 제어 ([Congestion Control](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/428_tcp_congestion_control_network_perspective/)) - 망(네트워크) 관점, 패킷 유실 방지

431. [임계치](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/431_ssthresh_slow_start_threshold/) (ssthresh, [Slow Start](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/430_slow_start_exponential_growth_cwnd/) Threshold) 
432. [혼잡 회피](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/432_congestion_avoidance_aimd_algorithm/) (Congestion Avoidance / AIMD [알고리즘](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/)) - CWND 선형 증가
433. [빠른 재전송](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/433_fast_retransmit_3_dup_ack/) ([Fast Retransmit](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/433_fast_retransmit_3_dup_ack/)) - 중복 ACK 3개(3 Dup-ACK) 수신 시 [타임아웃](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/09_cloud_native_ai_architecture/573_timeout_retry_backoff_strategy/) 전 재전송
434. [빠른 회복](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/434_fast_recovery_skip_slow_start/) ([Fast Recovery](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/434_fast_recovery_skip_slow_start/)) - 재전송 후 [슬로우 스타트](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/430_slow_start_exponential_growth_cwnd/) 생략하고 혼잡회피로 진입
435. [TCP Tahoe](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/435_tcp_tahoe_timeout_dup_ack_drop_to_1/) ([타임아웃](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/09_cloud_native_ai_architecture/573_timeout_retry_backoff_strategy/), 3 Dup-ACK 모두 1로 하락) 모델
436. [TCP Reno](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/436_tcp_reno_fast_retransmit_recovery/) ([빠른 재전송](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/433_fast_retransmit_3_dup_ack/)/[빠른 회복](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/434_fast_recovery_skip_slow_start/) 지원) 모델
437. [TCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/405_tcp_transmission_control_protocol_connection_oriented/) NewReno / SACK (선택적 [확인](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/12_testing_maintenance/396_validation/)응답 옵션, 블록 다중유실 [회복](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/04_transactions_concurrency/233_recovery_database_restoration_overview/))

[TCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/405_tcp_transmission_control_protocol_connection_oriented/) BIC / CUBIC - 현대 리눅스 [커널](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/01_overview_architecture/022_kernel_role/) 기본 [알고리즘](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/) (지수함수 기반 고속망 최적화)

[BBR](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/439_bbr_bottleneck_bandwidth_and_rtt_google_congestion_control/) ([Bottleneck](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/617_io_bottleneck/) [Bandwidth](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/140_bandwidth/) and Round-trip propagation time) - 구글, [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/)시간 기반 혼잡제어

[RTT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/441_rtt_round_trip_time_srtt_smoothed/) ([Round Trip Time](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/441_rtt_round_trip_time_srtt_smoothed/)), SRTT (Smoothed [RTT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/441_rtt_round_trip_time_srtt_smoothed/)) - 혼잡 제어 동적 타이머

[MPTCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/446_mptcp_multipath_tcp_handover/) ([Multipath TCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/446_mptcp_multipath_tcp_handover/)) - 다중 경로 [대역폭](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/140_bandwidth/) 결합, 모바일/Wi-Fi [핸드오버](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/556_handover_handoff_types_concept/) 무단절

[SCTP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/447_sctp_multi_stream_multi_homing_4way_handshake/) ([Stream](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/467_http2_stream_multiplexing_tcp_hol/) Control Transmission [Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/)) - [다중 스트림](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/09_file_system/560_multi_stream_file_fork_ads/), 멀티 호밍 (Multi-homing), 4단계 핸드셰이크([쿠키](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/475_cookie_local_state/)방식)

[RTCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/452_rtcp_rtp_control_protocol_monitoring/) ([RTP Control Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/452_rtcp_rtp_control_protocol_monitoring/)) - 품질 감시 [모니터](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/04_synchronization/229_monitor/)링

[QUIC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/454_quic_quick_udp_internet_connections/) (Quick [UDP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/406_udp_user_datagram_protocol_connectionless_fast/) Internet Connections) - 전송 계층 혁신 (멀티플렉싱, 0-[RTT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/441_rtt_round_trip_time_srtt_smoothed/)/1-[RTT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/441_rtt_round_trip_time_srtt_smoothed/) 핸드셰이크)

[TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/11_exam_summary/694_thread_local_storage_tls/) 1.3 기본 내장 - [보안성](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/283_security_tactics/)과 [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/) 단축 동시 확보

[HTTP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/461_http_stateless_connection_oriented/) ([HyperText Transfer Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/461_http_stateless_connection_oriented/)) 상태 비저장 ([Stateless](/knowledge-base/studynote/15_devops_sre/05_devsecops/239_stateless_redis/)), 연결형/비연결형 특징

467. [HTTP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/461_http_stateless_connection_oriented/)/2 스트림 ([Stream](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/467_http2_stream_multiplexing_tcp_hol/)) [다중화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/02_multiplexing_multiple_access/071_다중화_Multiplexing/) ([Multiplexing](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/02_multiplexing_multiple_access/071_다중화_Multiplexing/)) - [HOL](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/456_quic_hol_head_of_line_blocking_resolution/) 우회 (단, [TCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/405_tcp_transmission_control_protocol_connection_oriented/) HOL은 잔존)

[HTTP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/461_http_stateless_connection_oriented/)/2 헤더 [압축](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/06_memory_management/347_compaction/) (HPACK [알고리즘](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/) 활용)

[HTTP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/461_http_stateless_connection_oriented/)/3 특징 - [QUIC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/454_quic_quick_udp_internet_connections/) [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) 활용, 완전한 멀티플렉싱, QPACK [압축](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/06_memory_management/347_compaction/), 연결 [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/) 거의 없음
471. [HTTPS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/471_https_http_over_tls/) ([HTTP over TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/471_https_http_over_tls/)) - [기밀성](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/01_intro_principles/002_confidentiality/), [무결성](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/01_intro_principles/003_integrity/) 지원 ([포트](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/08_storage_and_io_systems/446_port_and_bus/) 443)

475. [쿠키](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/475_cookie_local_state/) ([Cookie](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/475_cookie_local_state/)) - 클라이언트 로컬 저장 상태 값
476. [세션](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/160_session_controlling_terminal/) ([Session](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/160_session_controlling_terminal/)) - 서버 측 상태 저장 값 (SID 발급)

[REST API](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/477_rest_api_architecture/) ([Representational State Transfer](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/477_rest_api_architecture/)) - 자원 중심 구조, [HTTP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/461_http_stateless_connection_oriented/) 메서드 매핑
478. [GraphQL](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/03_cloud_infrastructure/246_graphql_query_language_overfetching_solution/) - 메타 개발, 클라이언트 주도 [쿼리](/knowledge-base/studynote/10_ai/04_ai_ops_ethics/298_qkv_attention/) 언어, 오버패칭 해소
479. [gRPC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/479_grpc_protobuf_http2/) - 구글 개발, [Protocol Buffers](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/09_cloud_native_ai_architecture/535_sync_communication_rest_grpc/) (ProtoBuf), [HTTP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/461_http_stateless_connection_oriented/)/2 기반 [초고속](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/148_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc/) [RPC](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/126_rpc/), [서비스 메시](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/05_security_compliance/302_service_mesh_istio/)([MSA](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/15_advanced_topics/619_msa_traffic_hardware/))

[WEP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/580_wep_wired_equivalent_privacy_rc4/) ([Wired Equivalent Privacy](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/580_wep_wired_equivalent_privacy_rc4/)) - [RC4](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/02_crypto/081_rc4_stream_cipher/) 기반, 취약점(정적키) 노출
581. [WPA](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/581_wpa_tkip_802_1x_eap/) (TKIP + 802.[1X](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/584_802_1x_pnac_eap_radius/) + [EAP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/04_data_link_layer_error/229_eap_extensible_authentication_protocol/))
582. [WPA2](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/582_wpa2_aes_ccmp_personal_enterprise/) ([AES](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/656_aes_advanced_encryption_standard_rijndael/)-CCMP 기반) 강력 암호화, 개인용([PSK](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/142_psk_pre_shared_key/))/기업용(Enterprise/[RADIUS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/10_application_layer_dns_mgmt/541_radius_remote_authentication_aaa/))
583. [WPA3](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/583_wpa3_sae_owe_enhanced_open/) - SAE (Simultaneous [Authentication](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/604_authentication_factors/) of Equals) 핸드셰이크 도입 (오프라인 딕셔너리 공격 완전차단), 관리 프레임 [보호](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/571_protection_vs_security/) (PMF, 필수 적용), 192비트 기업급 보안 스위트(CNSA/Suite B)
584. 802.[1X](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/584_802_1x_pnac_eap_radius/) (PNAC, [Port](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/08_storage_and_io_systems/446_port_and_bus/) Based [Network Access Control](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/05_web_app_security/226_nac_network_access_control_ieee_802_1x/)) [인증](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/303_authentication_authorization_patterns/) 및 [EAP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/04_data_link_layer_error/229_eap_extensible_authentication_protocol/)/[RADIUS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/10_application_layer_dns_mgmt/541_radius_remote_authentication_aaa/) 체계
585. [캡티브 포털](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/585_captive_portal_guest_web_auth/) ([Captive Portal](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/585_captive_portal_guest_web_auth/)) - 게스트 웹 기반 접속 [인증](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/303_authentication_authorization_patterns/)
586. [안테나](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/03_physical_layer_media/171_antenna_basic_dipole_resonance/) 증폭 측정 지표: dBm 반값 전력각 등
587. [무선 메시 네트워크](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/587_wireless_mesh_network_daisy_chain/) ([Wireless Mesh Network](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/587_wireless_mesh_network_daisy_chain/)) - [데이지 체인](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/09_system_bus_interconnects/354_daisy_chain/) 구조 연결 자동화
588. [MANET](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/588_manet_mobile_ad_hoc_network/) ([Mobile Ad-hoc Network](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/588_manet_mobile_ad_hoc_network/)) - 기지국 없는 노드 기반 네트워크 [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) (AODV 규칙)
589. [V2X](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/141_v2x_vehicle_to_everything_communication/) ([Vehicle to Everything](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/589_v2x_vehicle_to_everything_autonomous/)) - 차량 자율주행, 차량통신 핵심
590. [WAVE](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/590_wave_ieee_802_11p_dsrc_v2x/) (IEEE 802.11p 무선차량통신) [DSRC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/1025_c_v2x_wave_dsrc/)(단거리전용)
591. [C-V2X](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/143_c_v2x_cellular_based_communication/) ([Cellular V2X](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/143_c_v2x_cellular_based_communication/)) - [3GPP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/751_3gpp_3rd_generation_partnership_project/) 표준, 이동통신([LTE](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/752_lte_long_term_evolution_4g/)/[5G](/knowledge-base/studynote/07_enterprise_systems/09_digital_transformation/418_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc_slicing/)) 연계 차량 통신
592. [위성 통신](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/592_satellite_communication_characteristics/) ([Satellite Comm](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/592_satellite_communication_characteristics/).) 특징
593. [정지 궤도 위성](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/593_geo_geostationary_earth_orbit_satellite/) ([GEO](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/593_geo_geostationary_earth_orbit_satellite/)) - 약 35,800km 고도 통신 위성 ([지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/) 심함)
594. [중궤도 위성](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/594_meo_medium_earth_orbit_gps/) ([MEO](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/594_meo_medium_earth_orbit_gps/)) (GPS, 항법시스템)
595. [저궤도 위성](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/595_leo_low_earth_orbit_starlink_6g/) ([LEO](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/595_leo_low_earth_orbit_starlink_6g/)) - 500~1500km 고도. 초저지연 글로벌 [6G](/knowledge-base/studynote/07_enterprise_systems/09_digital_transformation/419_6g_ntn_thz_ris_next_gen/) 망 구성, Starlink(스타링크), OneWeb(원웹)
596. [HAPS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/596_haps_high_altitude_platform_station_drone/) (고고도 전송 기지국, 성층권 드론 통신)
597. GPS (Global Positioning System) 삼각 측량 / 오차 개선 기법 (DGPS, RTK)
598. [UWB](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/160_uwb_ultra_wideband/) ([Ultra-Wideband](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/598_uwb_ultra_wideband_indoor_positioning/)) - 초광대역 근거리 정밀 위치인식통신, 임펄스 전송 (Apple AirTag 등)
599. 무선 충전 전송 원리 (자기 유도형 WPC Qi, 자기 공명형 A4WP)
600. RFID / NFC [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) 기본 구상

## 12. [사물인터넷](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/)([IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/)), [WPAN](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/604_wpan_wireless_personal_area_network/) 및 엣지 통신 (50개)
601. [사물인터넷](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) ([IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/), Internet of Things)의 3대 요소 (디바이스, 네트워크, 클라우드/플랫폼)
602. [사물 통신](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/602_m2m_machine_to_machine_telemetry/) ([M2M](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/602_m2m_machine_to_machine_telemetry/)) - 기기 간 직접 연결 (IoT의 근본)
603. [센서 네트워크](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/103_wsn_sensor_network/) ([WSN](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/103_wsn_sensor_network/), Wireless Sensor Network) / 싱크 노드 (Sink Node) 구성
604. [WPAN](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/604_wpan_wireless_personal_area_network/) (Wireless Personal Area Network) - 개인 작업공간 무선
605. [블루투스](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/605_bluetooth_ieee_802_15_1_piconet_scatternet/) ([Bluetooth](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/605_bluetooth_ieee_802_15_1_piconet_scatternet/)) - IEEE 802.15.1, ISM 대역(2.4GHz), 피코넷(Piconet), 스캐터넷(Scatternet) 마스터/슬레이브
606. [블루투스 버전](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/606_bluetooth_edr_hs_speed_extension/) - [EDR](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/04_endpoint_security/325_edr/), HS 속도 확장
607. [BLE](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/607_ble_bluetooth_low_energy_iot/) ([Bluetooth Low Energy](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/607_ble_bluetooth_low_energy_iot/)) - BT 4.0, 저전력 특화, [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) 핵심
608. [비컨](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/608_beacon_technology_ibeacon_eddystone/) ([Beacon](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/608_beacon_technology_ibeacon_eddystone/)) 기술 - iBeacon(애플 환경), Eddystone(오픈 소스 [블루투스](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/605_bluetooth_ieee_802_15_1_piconet_scatternet/) 로케이터)
609. [ZigBee](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/609_zigbee_ieee_802_15_4_mesh_iot/) ([지그비](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/609_zigbee_ieee_802_15_4_mesh_iot/)) - IEEE 802.15.4 초저전력 제어/감시용 메쉬 네트워킹 표준, 250Kbps
610. [Z-Wave](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/610_z_wave_900mhz_smart_home_iot/) (Z웨이브) - 홈네트워크 최적화 900MHz 무선 통신 [반도체](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/01_basic_electronics_logic/009_semiconductor/) 생태계 주도
611. [Thread](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/092_thread_lwp/) [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) - [IPv6](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/324_ipv6_128bit_next_generation_address/) 통신 기반 [메시](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/10_parallel_processing_architecture/389_mesh_topology/) [WPAN](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/604_wpan_wireless_personal_area_network/) [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) (스마트홈 [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) 지원)
612. [Matter](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/612_matter_csa_smart_home_standard/) ([매터](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/612_matter_csa_smart_home_standard/)) 보안 통일 표준(CSA) - 애플/구글/아마존 간 통일형 스마트홈 상호 운용성 표준 (위 Layer 적용)
613. [6LoWPAN](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/117_6lowpan_iot_ipv6/) - IEEE 802.15.4 환경의 저전력 장치를 IP 계층인 [IPv6](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/324_ipv6_128bit_next_generation_address/) 로 인터넷 연동시키는 헤더 [압축](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/06_memory_management/347_compaction/)/[단편화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/291_fragmentation_and_reassembly_process/) ([IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) 핵심)
614. [RPL](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/614_rpl_ipv6_routing_low_power_lossy/) ([IPv6](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/324_ipv6_128bit_next_generation_address/) [Routing](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) [Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) for Low-[Power](/knowledge-base/studynote/14_data_engineering/02_math_mining/069_type_1_2_error_statistical_power/) and Lossy Networks) - [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) 무선 최적 [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) [알고리즘](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/)
615. [LPWAN](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/109_lpwan_low_power_wide_area_network/) ([Low-Power Wide-Area Network](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/615_lpwan_low_power_wide_area_network/)) 개요 (저전력 광역 통신) - 수십km 커버리지
616. 비면허 대역 [LPWAN](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/109_lpwan_low_power_wide_area_network/) 분야
617. [LoRa](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/617_lora_lorawan_css_chirp_spread_spectrum/) ([Long Range](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/617_lora_lorawan_css_chirp_spread_spectrum/)) / LoRaWAN 표준 - [CSS](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/110_unlicensed_lpwan_lorawan_sigfox/) (Chirp [Spread Spectrum](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/068_스펙트럼_확산_Spread_Spectrum/)) 방식의 비면허 글로벌 저전력 장거리
618. [Sigfox](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/1030_lpwan_sigfox/) - 초협대역(UNB) 100bps 극초저전력, 소용량/프랑스 기반 상용화
619. 면허 대역 [LPWAN](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/109_lpwan_low_power_wide_area_network/) 분야 (이동통신사 기반형 [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/))
620. [NB-IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/620_nbiot_narrowband_iot_lte_guardband/) ([Narrowband IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/620_nbiot_narrowband_iot_lte_guardband/)) - [LTE](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/752_lte_long_term_evolution_4g/) 주파수 여유 대역/[보호](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/571_protection_vs_security/)대역 기반 200kHz 협대역 활용 [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) 표준 ([3GPP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/751_3gpp_3rd_generation_partnership_project/) Rel.13)
621. [LTE-M](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/621_ltem_emtc_iot_mobility_voice/) ([eMTC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/621_ltem_emtc_iot_mobility_voice/)) - Cat-M1 등 음성/1Mbps 이동([핸드오버](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/556_handover_handoff_types_concept/)) 및 웨어러블 지원 [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/)
622. [MQTT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/622_mqtt_publish_subscribe_qos/) (Message Queuing Telemetry Transport) - [TCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/405_tcp_transmission_control_protocol_connection_oriented/) 기반 퍼블리시-서브스크라이브 경량 [메시](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/10_parallel_processing_architecture/389_mesh_topology/)지 ([QoS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/388_qos_quality_of_service_best_effort_intserv_diffserv/) 0/1/2 등급 지원) 브로커(Broker) 중심 [분산](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/08_stats/136_variance/)형
623. [CoAP](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/120_coap_constrained_application_protocol/) ([Constrained Application Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/120_coap_constrained_application_protocol/)) - [UDP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/406_udp_user_datagram_protocol_connectionless_fast/) 제어 기반, [REST](/knowledge-base/studynote/07_enterprise_systems/03_eai_esb_msa/156_rest_representational_state_transfer/)/[HTTP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/461_http_stateless_connection_oriented/) 메타 대응 경량 [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/), 브로커리스 구조([DTLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/644_dtls_datagram_tls_coap_security/) 접속 지원)
624. [LwM2M](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/121_lwm2m_lightweight_m2m/) ([Lightweight M2M](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/121_lwm2m_lightweight_m2m/)) 표준 [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) 관리 메커니즘
625. oneM2M 아키텍처 (국제 표준 통합 [M2M](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/602_m2m_machine_to_machine_telemetry/) 구조화 플랫폼)
626. [엣지 컴퓨팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/05_security_compliance/235_edge_computing_smart_factory/) ([Edge Computing](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/05_security_compliance/235_edge_computing_smart_factory/), [포그 컴퓨팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/106_fog_computing_cisco_architecture/) 구분) - 종단 클라이언트 인접 노드 [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/) [분산](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/08_stats/136_variance/) 처리 방식
627. [MEC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/627_mec_multi_access_edge_computing_5g/) (Multi-access [Edge Computing](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/05_security_compliance/235_edge_computing_smart_factory/) / Mobile [Edge Computing](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/05_security_compliance/235_edge_computing_smart_factory/)) - [5G](/knowledge-base/studynote/07_enterprise_systems/09_digital_transformation/418_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc_slicing/), 이통망 기지국 근접하여 연산, 초저지연 확보
628. [스마트 그리드](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/161_smart_grid_architecture/) ([Smart Grid](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/161_smart_grid_architecture/) 파워 네트워크 통신 인프라)
629. [마이크로 그리드](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/629_microgrid_ami_smart_meter_plc_rf/) ([Microgrid](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/163_microgrid_island_mode/)) / [AMI](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/162_ami_advanced_metering_infrastructure/) (원격검침인프라) 통신 ([PLC](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/18_iot_ot_physical/896_plc_programmable_logic_controller/)/RF 장치) 탑재 방식
630. [산업용 이더넷 표준](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/630_industrial_ethernet_profinet_ethercat_modbus/) ([Industrial Ethernet](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/630_industrial_ethernet_profinet_ethercat_modbus/)) - [PROFINET](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/18_iot_ot_physical/900_profinet/), EtherCAT, Modbus [TCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/405_tcp_transmission_control_protocol_connection_oriented/) (타임크리티컬)
631. [OPC UA](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/631_opc_ua_smart_factory_protocol/) - [스마트 팩토리](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/166_smart_factory/), 산업용/제조용 디바이스 안전한 [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/) 통신 확장 [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) 통합
632. [TSN](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/15_advanced_topics/546_tsn_hardware/) ([Time-Sensitive Networking](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/168_industrial_ethernet_tsn/)) - IEEE 802.1 / 시간 결정형 유선 [이더넷](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/05_lan_wan_l2_devices/230_ethernet_structure_and_principles_ieee_802_3/), 정밀 동기(마이크로/나노단위 트래픽 자원 보장/[동기화](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/03_cpu_scheduling/212_synchronization_mechanisms/)) (산업용 [이더넷](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/05_lan_wan_l2_devices/230_ethernet_structure_and_principles_ieee_802_3/) 대체 5G망 연계)
633. [자율주행 차량 통신](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/633_v2v_v2i_autonomous_vehicle_communication/) (V2V, V2I 교통인프라 교환)
634. [OCF](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/123_ocf_open_connectivity_foundation/) ([Open Connectivity Foundation](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/123_ocf_open_connectivity_foundation/)) [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) 통합 관리 계층 표준
635. [IETF](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/635_ietf_core_working_group_coap/) (Internet 엔진ering [Task](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/150_task/) Force) 산하 [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) CoRE 워킹그룹 동향
636. [W3C WoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/636_wot_web_of_things_w3c_thing_description/) ([Web of Things](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/636_wot_web_of_things_w3c_thing_description/)) - 모든 기기를 URL 자원으로 통합 제어 개념
637. [IIoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/637_iiot_industrial_iot_qos_latency/) (공업계 [사물인터넷](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/)/산업용 [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/)) 트래픽 관리 한계/[QoS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/388_qos_quality_of_service_best_effort_intserv_diffserv/) 이슈
638. [스마트 시티](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/171_smart_city_platform_architecture/) ([Smart City](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/171_smart_city_platform_architecture/) 통신망 [다중화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/02_multiplexing_multiple_access/071_다중화_Multiplexing/) 연계) 센싱 시스템
639. 드론 통신 [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/)시간 관리 및 보안 [C2](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/15_malware_attack_vectors/746_c2/) 링크 ([Command & Control](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/639_drone_c2_link_command_control_latency/))
640. [AIoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/640_aiot_ai_and_iot_edge_cloud_latency/) ([AI](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/03_design_architecture/190_ai_llm_requirements_specification/) + [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/)) 모델 및 클라우드 [AI](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/03_design_architecture/190_ai_llm_requirements_specification/) 연결 [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/) 완화 기술
641. 홈 네트워크 게이트웨이 / 월패드 [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) 보안 (RS-485 해킹, 분리 [정책](/knowledge-base/studynote/10_ai/02_dl_architecture_new/164_policy/) 논란)
642. [망분리](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/05_security_compliance/182_network_separation_model/) ([Network Separation](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/05_security_compliance/182_network_separation_model/)) 및 [제로 트러스트](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/667_zero_trust_runtime_integrity_measurement/) 연결형 [논리](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/04_endpoint_security/369_logic_bomb/)망 [보안 정책](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/01_intro_principles/007_security_policy/)
643. 기기 간 상호인증체계 관리 기법 [P2P](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/18_optical_nextgen_automation/916_p2p_peer_to_peer_networking_super_node_gnutella/) 연결 [인증](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/303_authentication_authorization_patterns/)서 배포 기술
644. [DTLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/644_dtls_datagram_tls_coap_security/) ([Datagram TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/644_dtls_datagram_tls_coap_security/)) [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) [CoAP](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/120_coap_constrained_application_protocol/) 결합
645. 소형 [안테나](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/03_physical_layer_media/171_antenna_basic_dipole_resonance/) 시스템/초소형 센서 [백스캐터 통신](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/175_ambient_backscatter_communication/) (Ambient Backscatter 통신, 에너지 하베스팅)
646. [무전원 통신](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/646_passive_iot_intermittent_computing/) (Passive [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) 통신) 환경 적응
647. [CPS](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/167_cps_cyber_physical_system/) (Cyber-Physical System 트윈/메타 [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/) 전송 요구사항)
648. [양방향 스마트 계량기](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/12_iot_wpan_edge/648_smart_meter_two_way_communication_ami/) (Smart Meter 통신 규격)
649. 홈넷/[IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) [봇넷](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/19_frequent_topics_terms/990_botnet_cnc/) 방어 기법 (Mirai [Botnet](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/19_frequent_topics_terms/990_botnet_cnc/) DDOS 예방 [포트](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/08_storage_and_io_systems/446_port_and_bus/) 필터)
650. 저전력/메모리 한계 환경 경량 [대칭키 암호](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/02_crypto/076_symmetric_encryption/) (LEA 표준, ARIA 등 최적화 적용)

## 13. [네트워크 보안](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/20_performance_evaluation_advanced/1117_network_security_zero_trust_policy/) (기본 기법) (50개)
651. [정보보안 3대 요소](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/651_cia_triad_confidentiality_integrity_availability/) (CIA 트라이어드: [기밀성](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/01_intro_principles/002_confidentiality/), [무결성](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/01_intro_principles/003_integrity/), [가용성](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/13_reliability_power_management/452_availability/)) + [인증](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/303_authentication_authorization_patterns/), 부인방지 요구
652. [암호학](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/652_cryptography_concept_encryption_decryption/) ([Cryptography](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/652_cryptography_concept_encryption_decryption/)) 개요 통신망 보안 적용 (평문->암호문->평문 변환 체계)
653. [대칭키 암호화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/653_symmetric_key_cryptography_fast_speed/) ([Symmetric Key](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/653_symmetric_key_cryptography_fast_speed/)) - 암/복호화 키 동일, 공유기밀 분배, 고속 처리
654. [스트림 암호](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/654_stream_cipher_rc4_chacha20/) ([Stream Cipher](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/654_stream_cipher_rc4_chacha20/)) - [RC4](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/02_crypto/081_rc4_stream_cipher/) ([WEP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/580_wep_wired_equivalent_privacy_rc4/) 등, 최신 사장), ChaCha20
655. [블록 암호](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/655_block_cipher_des_3des_feistel/) ([Block Cipher](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/655_block_cipher_des_3des_feistel/)) - [DES](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/02_crypto/086_des_data_encryption_standard/) (56bit 비권장), [3DES](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/02_crypto/087_3des/) (과도기) 모델
656. [AES](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/656_aes_advanced_encryption_standard_rijndael/) ([Advanced Encryption Standard](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/656_aes_advanced_encryption_standard_rijndael/)) - 국제 대표 표준, Rijndael 구조 (128/192/256bit 체계)
657. SEED, ARIA, LEA - 대한민국의 표준 블록/경량 암호 방식 체계
658. [블록 암호](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/655_block_cipher_des_3des_feistel/) 운영 모드 (ECB 기본/취약 모드, [CBC](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/02_crypto/089_cbc_mode/)([IV](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/288_version_ihl_tos_total_length/) 필요), CFB, OFB, [CTR](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/02_crypto/090_ctr_mode/))
659. [GCM](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/659_gcm_galois_counter_mode_aead/) (Galois/[Counter](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/01_basic_electronics_logic/059_counter/) Mode) 모드 - 암호화와 더불어 [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/) [인증](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/303_authentication_authorization_patterns/) 기능 탑재 ([AEAD](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/02_crypto/092_aead/) - [TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/11_exam_summary/694_thread_local_storage_tls/) 1.3의 핵심 모드)
660. 비대칭키/공개키 암호화 (Asymmetric/Public [Key](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/02_modeling_normalization/067_db_key_uniqueness_minimality/)) - 암/복호화 키 상이, 서명(개인키)/기밀화(공개키 전송) 가능
661. [수학적 문제 기반](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/661_asymmetric_key_math_factorization_dlp/)(소인수분해, 이산대수 등)
662. [RSA](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/110_rsa/) [알고리즘](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/) - 가장 보편적 (소인수분해 수학 난해성, 2048bit 권장)
663. ElGamal 및 DSA ([디지털 서명용 특화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/663_elgamal_dsa_discrete_logarithm_digital_signature/)) 시스템
664. [ECC](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/15_advanced_topics/554_ecc_circuit/) (Elliptical Curve [Cryptography](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/652_cryptography_concept_encryption_decryption/), 타원 곡선 통신망 적용) - 짧은 키 (256bit)로 [RSA](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/110_rsa/) 3072bit 효과 발휘 ([초고속](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/148_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc/), 모바일/[IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) 적용)
665. [ECDSA](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/01_blockchain/097_ecdsa_schnorr_signature_bitcoin/), Ed25519 (고성능 차세대 공개키 디지털 [전자서명](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/675_digital_signature_process_asymmetric_key/) 방식)
666. 디피-헬만 상호 키 교환 ([Diffie-Hellman Key Exchange](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/666_diffie_hellman_key_exchange_mitm/)) 원리 및 스니핑 취약점
667. [해시 함수](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/667_hash_function_integrity_one_way/) ([Hash Function](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/667_hash_function_integrity_one_way/)) - [무결성](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/01_intro_principles/003_integrity/) 점검을 위한 일방향 고정길이 [압축](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/06_memory_management/347_compaction/)
668. [MD5](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/668_md5_hash_collision_vulnerability/) (취약성/충돌 노출) 회피 조치, SHA-1 차단
669. SHA-2 패밀리 - SHA-256 / SHA-512 위주 통신망 서명 기본 기술
670. [SHA-3](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/02_crypto/101_sha_3/) 패밀리 - 스펀지(Sponge) 펑션 방식 [Keccak](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/02_crypto/101_sha_3/) 로직, 기존 방어 [결함](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/06_software_architecture/352_defect_definition/) 해소
671. [솔트](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/671_password_hash_salt_pbkdf2_bcrypt_argon2/) ([Salt](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/671_password_hash_salt_pbkdf2_bcrypt_argon2/)) 첨가 패스워드 해시 (PBKDF2, bcrypt, Argon2) 체계 - 레인보우 테이블 방지
672. [무결성](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/01_intro_principles/003_integrity/) 및 출처 [인증](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/303_authentication_authorization_patterns/)용 서명 [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/) 코드 제어
673. [MAC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/673_mac_message_authentication_code/) ([Message Authentication Code](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/673_mac_message_authentication_code/)) 변수 및 기능
674. [HMAC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/674_hmac_hash_based_mac_ipsec/) ([Hash-based MAC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/674_hmac_hash_based_mac_ipsec/)) 통신 기반 [IPsec](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/15_advanced_topics/589_ipsec_offload/) 등 활용 구조 - 공유키 결합 해시
675. [전자서명](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/675_digital_signature_process_asymmetric_key/) ([Digital Signature](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/675_digital_signature_process_asymmetric_key/)) [생성](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/087_process_state_transition/)/[검증](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/07_object_oriented/395_verification_process_review/) 프로세스 개요 (비대칭키 활용 체계의 [무결성](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/01_intro_principles/003_integrity/) 보증)
676. [공개키 기반 구조](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/676_pki_public_key_infrastructure/) ([PKI](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/159_pki_public_key_infrastructure/), [Public Key Infrastructure](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/uncategorized/984_pki_public_key_infrastructure_ca_ra_certificate/)) 아키텍처 보안 증명 시스템
677. [인증국](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/677_ca_ra_certificate_authority_registration/) ([CA](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/01_blockchain/089_contract_account_smart_contract/), Certificate Authority), 등록기관 ([RA](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/161_ra_registration_authority/), [Registration Authority](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/161_ra_registration_authority/)), 저장소 체계
678. [CRL](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/678_crl_certificate_revocation_list/) ([Certificate Revocation List](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/678_crl_certificate_revocation_list/)) 스펙 및 폐기 문제 및 배포 [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/) 약점 완화 체계
679. [OCSP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/679_ocsp_online_certificate_status_protocol/) (Online Certificate Status [Protocol](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/)) - 실시간 [인증](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/303_authentication_authorization_patterns/)서 상태 응답 검사 체계
680. [OCSP Stapling](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/680_ocsp_stapling_tls_handshake_performance/) ([TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/11_exam_summary/694_thread_local_storage_tls/) Handshake 트래픽 [성능](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/282_performance_tactics/) 확장용 서버 캐시 상태 전송 메커니즘 개선기법)
681. SSL/[TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/11_exam_summary/694_thread_local_storage_tls/) (Secure [Socket](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/125_socket/) Layer / Transport Layer [Security](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/283_security_tactics/)) 통신 모델 개요 
682. [TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/11_exam_summary/694_thread_local_storage_tls/) Handshake [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) (3-Way 유사 연결 [초기](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/459_quic_fec_forward_error_correction/)화, [세션](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/160_session_controlling_terminal/)키 협상, Cipher Suite 교환 포함)

[TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/11_exam_summary/694_thread_local_storage_tls/) 전방향 안전성 (PFS, Perfect [Forward](/knowledge-base/studynote/10_ai/03_llm_nlp/235_forward_backward_chaining/) Secrecy) 보장 원리 ([RSA](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/110_rsa/) 직접 복호 문제 해결/임시 [세션](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/160_session_controlling_terminal/)키)

[TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/11_exam_summary/694_thread_local_storage_tls/) 1.3 업그레이드 변화와 0-[RTT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/441_rtt_round_trip_time_srtt_smoothed/)/1-[RTT](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/441_rtt_round_trip_time_srtt_smoothed/) [성능](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/282_performance_tactics/) 향상 차이

[세션 재개](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/687_tls_session_resumption_ticket/) ([Session](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/160_session_controlling_terminal/) Resumption / [TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/11_exam_summary/694_thread_local_storage_tls/) Ticket) 기능 구성
688. [SNI](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/688_sni_esni_ech_encrypted_client_hello/) ([Server Name Indication](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/688_sni_esni_ech_encrypted_client_hello/)) 개요 와 [ESNI](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/20_performance_evaluation_advanced/1064_esni_ech_tls_1_3_encrypted_sni/) / ECH (Encrypted [Client](/knowledge-base/studynote/11_design_supervision/01_audit_framework/003_audit_stakeholders/) Hello) 검열 우회

[양자 내성 암호](/knowledge-base/studynote/14_data_engineering/04_mlops/183_post_quantum_cryptography_key_transition/) ([PQC](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/05_security_compliance/351_quantum_computing_pqc_transition/), [Post-Quantum Cryptography](/knowledge-base/studynote/14_data_engineering/04_mlops/183_post_quantum_cryptography_key_transition/)) 체계 및 통신망 교환 표준 (Shor's [Algorithm](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/01_basics/001_algorithm_definition/) 위협 대처)

691. [패킷 필터](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/691_packet_filter_application_proxy/) (Packet Filter 라우터/L3,L4), 애플리케이션 상태 필터 및 [프록시](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/04_testing_quality/264_proxy_pattern_surrogate_access_control/)

[상태 기반 감시](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/692_stateful_inspection_firewall_principle/) ([Stateful Inspection](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/19_frequent_topics_terms/992_firewall_stateful_inspection/) / [세션](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/160_session_controlling_terminal/) 테이블 체크 메모리) 기술의 원리
693. [NIDS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/693_nids_network_intrusion_detection_system/) (Network [Intrusion Detection System](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/uncategorized/994_ids_ips_intrusion_detection_prevention_false_positive/) [분산](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/08_stats/136_variance/) 탐지) 공격
694. [스노트](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/694_snort_suricata_misuse_anomaly_detection/) ([Snort](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/694_snort_suricata_misuse_anomaly_detection/)), [Suricata](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/05_web_app_security/240_suricata_multithreaded_nids_ids_ips_engine/) ([병렬](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/07_exam_summary/430_index_fast_full_scan/) 룰 지원) 와 오용 탐지(Misuse) vs [이상 탐지](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/05_web_app_security/236_anomaly_based_detection_zero_day_false_positive/)([Anomaly](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/04_transactions_concurrency/530_anomaly/)) 엔진

696. [WAF](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/696_waf_web_application_firewall/) ([Web Application Firewall](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/05_web_app_security/242_waf_web_application_firewall_l7_protection/), 애플리케이션L7 특화)
697. [UTM](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/147_utm_unmanned_aircraft_system_traffic_management/) (Unified Threat [Management](/knowledge-base/studynote/12_it_management/05_security_compliance/372_management/) 패키징 통합형 장비)
698. [NGFW](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/698_ngfw_next_generation_firewall/) (Next-Generation [Firewall](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/690_firewall_generation_evolution/), [차세대 방화벽](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/05_web_app_security/216_ngfw_next_generation_firewall_dpi/) 시그니처 융합 모델 딥 패킷 파싱 애플리케이션 ID 제어 적용)

[NAC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/700_nac_network_access_control/) ([Network Access Control](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/05_web_app_security/226_nac_network_access_control_ieee_802_1x/) 시스템) 내부 접근 단말기 관리 [무결성](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/01_intro_principles/003_integrity/) 진단

## 14. [네트워크 보안](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/20_performance_evaluation_advanced/1117_network_security_zero_trust_policy/) (공격 및 위협, 대응책) (50개)
701. [도청](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/14_network_security_threats/701_sniffing_eavesdropping_promiscuous/) (Sniffing, Eavesdropping) 네트워크 토폴로지 취약 (프론미스큐어스 모드, [스위치](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/05_lan_wan_l2_devices/238_switch_operation_principles/) 잼 체계 해소)
