703. [ARP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/312_arp_address_resolution_protocol_ip_to_mac/) [스푸핑](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/598_spoofing/) ([ARP Spoofing](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/19_frequent_topics_terms/991_arp_spoofing/)) - [MAC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/673_mac_message_authentication_code/) 주소 기만 타겟 [PC](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/04_instruction_set_architecture/164_pc/) 트래픽 탈취, 중간자 정적대응 방어 (Static [ARP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/312_arp_address_resolution_protocol_ip_to_mac/))
704. IP [스푸핑](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/598_spoofing/) ([IP Spoofing](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/14_network_security_threats/704_ip_spoofing_trust_injection/)) - 트러스트 IP 위장 [인젝션](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/11_testing_validation/480_injection/) 우회 및 DDoS 공격 근원
705. [DNS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/10_application_layer_dns_mgmt/511_dns_hierarchical_distributed_architecture/) [스푸핑](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/598_spoofing/) / [DNS Cache Poisoning](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/265_dns_cache_poisoning/) 매칭 [결함](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/06_software_architecture/352_defect_definition/) [포트 번호](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/402_port_number_16bit_application_process_identification/) 난수 제어 취약 노출 방어 기법 ([DNSSEC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/10_application_layer_dns_mgmt/518_dnssec_dns_security_extensions/) 도입 목적)
706. [중간자 공격](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/14_network_security_threats/706_mitm_man_in_the_middle_hsts/) (MitM, Man-in-the-Middle) [도청](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/14_network_security_threats/701_sniffing_eavesdropping_promiscuous/) 흐름과 통제 조치 ([TLS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/11_exam_summary/694_thread_local_storage_tls/) 암호 [검증](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/07_object_oriented/395_verification_process_review/) 중요성, [HSTS](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/05_web_app_security/268_hsts/) [설정](/knowledge-base/studynote/15_devops_sre/01_culture_methodology/009_config/) 이유)
707. [세션 하이재킹](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/14_network_security_threats/707_session_hijacking_tcp_seq_cookie/) ([Session Hijacking](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/271_session_hijacking/) / [TCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/405_tcp_transmission_control_protocol_connection_oriented/) Seq 번호 추정 절도 등 탈취/[Cookie](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/475_cookie_local_state/)/토큰 갈취 기법)
708. [재생 공격](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/14_network_security_threats/708_replay_attack_timestamp_nonce/) ([Replay Attack](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/274_replay_attack/) 방어 타임스탬프 원리 / 비표 넌스 [Nonce](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/05_web_app_security/519_oidc_nonce/) 적용)
709. [DoS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/599_dos_ddos_attack/) (Denial of [Service](/knowledge-base/studynote/13_cloud_architecture/02_iaas_paas_saas/090_service_kubernetes_network_load_balancing/) [가용성](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/13_reliability_power_management/452_availability/) 타격 위협 목적) 
710. [분산](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/08_stats/136_variance/) [서비스 거부 공격](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/19_frequent_topics_terms/989_dos_denial_of_service/) (DDoS, Distributed [DoS](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/599_dos_ddos_attack/) 위협) [봇넷](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/19_frequent_topics_terms/990_botnet_cnc/) 시스템 C&C 서버 증폭, 감염 및 반사 
711. [SYN Flood](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/255_syn_flood/) 공격 ([TCP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/08_transport_layer/405_tcp_transmission_control_protocol_connection_oriented/) 3way-Handshake 약점 Backlog 큐 포화 자원 마비 유도)
712. [SYN Flood](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/255_syn_flood/) 대응 - SYN [Cookie](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/475_cookie_local_state/) 기술 (상태비저장 SYN/ACK 암호화 처리 후 최종 ACK서 [검증](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/07_object_oriented/395_verification_process_review/)) 서버 완화 제어

714. Ping of Death 대형 패킷 [단편화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/291_fragmentation_and_reassembly_process/) [버퍼 오버플로우](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/10_security/591_buffer_overflow/) 문제
715. TearDrop 공격 (IP 헤더 오프셋 중복/오류 [단편화](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/291_fragmentation_and_reassembly_process/) 무한 재조립 오류 기만 다운)
716. [UDP Flood](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/256_udp_flood/) 리소스 고갈 유도 / Null/Christmas Tree [플래그](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/04_data_link_layer_error/186_character_stuffing_dle_stx_etx/) 비대칭공격 타격

718. [NTP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/10_application_layer_dns_mgmt/536_ntp_network_time_protocol_stratum/) 증폭 (monlist [모니터](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/04_synchronization/229_monitor/) 목록 명령 악용/수백배 반사)
719. [DNS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/10_application_layer_dns_mgmt/511_dns_hierarchical_distributed_architecture/) 증폭 (위장 IP로 파싱 [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/)/TXT 등 다량 요구 패킷 대형화 수백배 반사 대상자 타격)

721. SLOW GET / SLOW POST 공격([Slowloris](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/03_network_security/258_slowloris/)) 응용 계층 [소켓](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/125_socket/) 점유, 헤더/엔티티를 끊임없이 매우 느리게 보내 Connection 지속 자원 무력화 (일시적 [방화벽](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/13_network_security_basics/690_firewall_generation_evolution/) 필터링, [타임아웃](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/09_cloud_native_ai_architecture/573_timeout_retry_backoff_strategy/) 최소화 대응)  
722. [트래픽 혼잡공격](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/14_network_security_threats/722_slowloris_http_get_delay_attack/) ([CC](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/17_framework_compliance/883_common_criteria_iso_15408/) Attack [봇넷](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/19_frequent_topics_terms/990_botnet_cnc/) [HTTP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/09_application_layer_web_email/461_http_stateless_connection_oriented/) 임의페이지 무한 요청) 유도 및 캡챠 적용
723. [랜섬웨어](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/15_malware_attack_vectors/730_ransomware/) ([Ransomware](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/15_malware_attack_vectors/730_ransomware/)) [파일](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/09_file_system/501_file_definition_logical_record/)공유 139/445망 자가전파 및 [피싱](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/15_malware_attack_vectors/752_phishing/) 방어 [포트](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/08_storage_and_io_systems/446_port_and_bus/) 폐쇄 규약 체계 SMBv1 보안 약점 타격 (워너크라이 [WannaCry](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/15_malware_attack_vectors/732_wannacry/) 분석) 
724. 다크 웹 (Dark Web) Tor (The Onion Router 은닉망 릴레이 체계 분석)

## 15. 차세대 통신망 (4G/[5G](/knowledge-base/studynote/07_enterprise_systems/09_digital_transformation/418_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc_slicing/)/[6G](/knowledge-base/studynote/07_enterprise_systems/09_digital_transformation/419_6g_ntn_thz_ris_next_gen/)) 핵심 아키텍처 (50개)
751. [3GPP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/751_3gpp_3rd_generation_partnership_project/) (3rd Generation Partnership [Project](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/042_relational_algebra_project/)) 표준 개발
752. [LTE](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/752_lte_long_term_evolution_4g/) (Long Term Evolution 4세대 망 진화) All-IP [패킷 교환](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/05_lan_wan_l2_devices/276_packet_switching_vs_circuit_switching_message_switching/) 완전 전환, [OFDMA](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/19_frequent_topics_terms/945_ofdma_orthogonal_frequency_division_multiple_access_resource_block/)

754. [MME](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/754_mme_mobility_management_entity/) ([Mobility Management](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/561_mobility_management_hlr_vlr_paging/) Entity 제어 [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/) 평면 구조적 통제 [핸드오버](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/11_wireless_mobile_communication/556_handover_handoff_types_concept/))
755. [HSS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/755_hss_home_subscriber_server/) (Home Subscriber Server 가입자 마스터 정보)
756. 기지국: eNodeB [분산](/knowledge-base/studynote/08_algorithm_stats/08_stats/136_variance/) [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/) 평면 [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) 고속망 이동성 통제 진화
757. [LTE-A](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/757_ltea_carrier_aggregation/) ([LTE-Advanced](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/757_ltea_carrier_aggregation/)) [캐리어 어그리게이션](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/20_performance_evaluation_advanced/1014_carrier_aggregation_lte_advanced_5g/) (Carrier Aggregation [CA](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/01_blockchain/089_contract_account_smart_contract/) 2~5개 주파수 대역 묶음 전송 캐파 향상 제어 속도 증강 기술 규격 제정 진전 표준 체계 진화)
758. [VoLTE](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/758_volte_voice_over_lte_sip_qos/) (Voice over [LTE](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/752_lte_long_term_evolution_4g/) 음성 통화 올 IP 패킷망 진화 우선 제어 처리 [SIP](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/15_advanced_topics/535_system_in_package/) [QOS](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/388_qos_quality_of_service_best_effort_intserv_diffserv/) 제어망 적용 구조 최적화)
759. [5G](/knowledge-base/studynote/07_enterprise_systems/09_digital_transformation/418_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc_slicing/) 통신 [성능](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/282_performance_tactics/) 목표 3대 특징 ([초고속](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/148_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc/), 초연결, 초저지연) 기능적 체계 진화 특징 비교 
760. [eMBB](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/760_embb_enhanced_mobile_broadband_vr_ar/) (Enhanced Mobile Broadband [초고속](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/148_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc/) 광대역 대용량 증강 기술 적용) AR/VR 기술 지원 파급 체계 지원
761. [uRLLC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/761_urllc_ultra_reliable_low_latency/) (Ultra-Reliable and Low [Latency](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/141_latency/) Communications 초안정/초고신뢰 초저지연망 차량 제어/[스마트 팩토리](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/166_smart_factory/) 통신 [프로토콜](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/06_network_layer_ip/295_protocol_field_tcp_udp_icmp/) 설계 1ms) 
762. [mMTC](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/762_mmtc_massive_machine_type_communications/) (Massive Machine-Type Communications 초거대 밀도 초다수 연결 사물 기기 [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) 연결망 배터리 저전력 제어 적용 모델 [성능](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/05_devops_ci_cd/282_performance_tactics/) 지표 적용)
763. [5G NR](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/763_5g_nr_new_radio_scalable_numerology/) ([New Radio](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/15_nextgen_communication_architecture/763_5g_nr_new_radio_scalable_numerology/)) 신무선 표준 대역
764. FR1 주파수 (Sub-6GHz 대역, 기존 호환 및 중간 광역/보편 속도 모델 적용 제어) 
765. FR2 주파수 ([mmWave](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/03_physical_layer_media/156_mmwave_millimeter_wave/) 24Ghz~ [밀리미터파](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/03_physical_layer_media/156_mmwave_millimeter_wave/) 직진성 극한, 장애물 회절 약화 대형 [스몰셀](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/03_physical_layer_media/178_small_cell_macro_femto/) 조밀 구성 기술 체계 대역) 
766. 793. 양자 인터넷 [모듈](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/04_testing_quality/192_module_independence/) 기반 네트워크 키 분배 안정성 [QKD](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/18_optical_nextgen_automation/922_qkd_quantum_key_distribution_bb84_eavesdropping/) 적용 ([양자 얽힘](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/03_cloud_infrastructure/220_quantum_entanglement/)/[복제](/knowledge-base/studynote/14_data_engineering/01_infrastructure/016_replication_factor/) 불가능 원리 광파장 탑재 보안 구간 해킹 원천 차단 무선 통신 기기망 연동 체계 구조 방식 인텔 암호 보장 릴레이망 구축)  
794. 프라이빗 5G망 (특화망 e-UM [5G](/knowledge-base/studynote/07_enterprise_systems/09_digital_transformation/418_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc_slicing/) 개념 적용 산업 공장 자체 구축망 라이센스 주파수 사설 구성망 비용 구조 보안 [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/) 한계 탈피망 맞춤형 [슬라이스](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/06_dw_olap_trends/331_neuromorphic_ai_db/) 대안 구조 모델 요약 정리)
795. [5G](/knowledge-base/studynote/07_enterprise_systems/09_digital_transformation/418_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc_slicing/) LAN [스위치](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/05_lan_wan_l2_devices/238_switch_operation_principles/) 대체 [이더넷](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/05_lan_wan_l2_devices/230_ethernet_structure_and_principles_ieee_802_3/) 투명 연계형 산업망 구축용 모델 브릿지 구성 기술 (L2 무결 연동 통신망 호환 제어망 융합 구성 요지 모델망 구성망 [프로비저닝](/knowledge-base/studynote/09_security/11_iam_access_control/528_provisioning/) 구조 체계 정리) 
796. 홀로그램 무선 전송 [압축](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/06_memory_management/347_compaction/)/다시점 비디오 체계 동기망 지터 제어 기술(VTC [지연](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/01_data_communication/015_지연_데이터_관점/) 민감 체계) 통신망 요구 지표 한계 모델 구조 모델 도입 요망 기술 요소)
797. 텔레햅틱 체계 기술 네트워크 [응답 시간](/knowledge-base/studynote/01_computer_architecture/03_architecture_basics_performance/138_response_time/)/제어 피드백 (Tactile Internet 극한 응답 한계 분석 및 신뢰 통신 5ms 이내 물리 제어 [데이터](/knowledge-base/studynote/05_database/01_db_architecture_relational/001_dikw_pyramid/) 패킷 순서 보장 모델 통신 기반 연계 인프라 방식 구성 기초 체재) 
798. 메이커 빔 [생성](/knowledge-base/studynote/02_operating_system/02_process_thread/087_process_state_transition/) [안테나](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/03_physical_layer_media/171_antenna_basic_dipole_resonance/) 메타 표면 적용 무전원 [IoT](/knowledge-base/studynote/06_ict_convergence/02_iot_mobility/101_iot_concept/) 환경 센서 연계 통신 시스템 구조 파악 설계 통신 방향 제안 
799. 동적 스펙트럼 공유 기술 (DSS 진화 4G/[5G](/knowledge-base/studynote/07_enterprise_systems/09_digital_transformation/418_5g_embb_urllc_mmtc_slicing/) 주파수 시간 단위 혼용 운영 자원 배분 유연성 통신 기술 방식 도입 전파 배급 한계 돌파 통계망 모델)  
800. 주파수 집성 기술 고급 모델 연대 전방위 고밀도 셀 간 간섭 회피 [CoMP](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/20_performance_evaluation_advanced/1013_comp_coordinated_multipoint_transmission/)(상호협력 통신 체계 전파 최적화 망)

[트래픽 섀도잉](/knowledge-base/studynote/15_devops_sre/03_sre_observability/167_traffic_shadowing_sre_testing/) ([Traffic Shadowing](/knowledge-base/studynote/15_devops_sre/03_sre_observability/167_traffic_shadowing_sre_testing/)) 및 [카나리 배포](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/02_requirements_analysis/115_canary_deployment_gradual_rollout/) 네트워킹 [라우팅](/knowledge-base/studynote/03_network/07_network_layer_routing/339_routing_overview_best_path_selection/) [전략](/knowledge-base/studynote/04_software_engineering/04_testing_quality/268_strategy_pattern/) (가치 테스트망) 분배 제어
